Need next_fast_len for length. We can implement power of 2:
```
def fft_len(length):
    M=1
    while M < length: M <<=1
    return M
```

- gradient_from_q(q, a, M, n): using FFT corr as above. Need reverse q. Could precompute? No q changes. Implementation:
```
def multiply_gradient(q, a, M, n):
    # q length 2n-1
    # Want full conv a * q[::-1]
    f_a = fft(a, M)
    f_q = fft(q[::-1], M)
    conv = ifft(f_a * f_q).real
    idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    return 2.0 * conv[idx]
```
But doing fft(a) each time. a changes. Could cache? q length large. Could use rfft for real to halve? numpy rfft. Use np.fft.rfft. For a and qrev real. M power2. f_a = np.fft.rfft(a, M), f_q = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], M), conv = np.fft.irfft(f_a*f_q, M).real. Good.

def normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.full(n, S/n)
    return a*(S/s)
```

Let's design `run_adam_y(a, tau_hi,tau_lo,lr0,budget, verbose)`:

  - tau schedule.
  - p = softmax(y) (max-subtract). Ensure sum: p = exp(y-y.max); p/=p.sum. a=S*p.

  - Normalize g by scale: scale = max(g.max(), abs(g.min()), 1e-12). g_scaled = g/scale. (or divide by p? no). Then `grad_y = p * g_scaled`. Because g_y = p*(G-mean)*constant. Scale absorbed in lr.
  - t++; m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad_y; v = beta2*v +(1-beta2)*grad_y^2.
  - mhat=m/(1-beta1^t); vhat=sqrt(v/(1-beta2^t))+eps.
  - step = lr * mhat/vhat. Clip step to [-max_step, max_step], max_step maybe 0.5? Since y step. y -= step.
  - y -= y.mean(); if max-min>50, rescale y? Rescaling changes p; maybe do if >50: y = 50 * y / (max-min) ? That is not just translation; changes distribution. Could avoid by adaptive lr small. Use step clip 1. If y range >30, renormalize y by clipping? We can set y = np.clip(y, -20, 20) relative? But changes. Maybe keep y raw; step clip 0.05? For p changes.
  - Evaluate score every step from c.
  - Adaptive lr: keep best_F; if F < last_F: lr*=1.02 else lr*=0.95. Or if max_c improving. Since F with changing tau not comparable. Use max_c. Track recent improvements.
- Return best a.

```
def init_root(n, target=None, max_iter=None):
    if target is None: target=np.ones(n)
    a=np.zeros(n)
    a[0]=sqrt(max(target[0], tiny))
    if n==1: return a
    # O(n^2) direct recurrence; can use FFT for cross? For one init n=4096 O(8M) okay.
    for k in 1..n-1:
        # cross = sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}; Note convolution coefficient c_k = a0 a_k + a_k a0 + cross.
        # For k up to n-1, all indices within.
        cross = np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])? Let's check: ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}. For i=1 -> a1*a_{k-1}; i=k-1 ->a_{k-1}*a1. Dot a[1:k] with a[k-1:0:-1] (a[k-1],...,a[1]) gives ordered pairs, correct.
        val=(target[k] - cross)/(2*a[0])
        if val<0: val=0 (or small)
        a[k]=val
    return a
```

```
def soft_weights(c, tau):
    c = c - c.max()
    # if tau extremely small, use top few
    exp_c = np.exp(c / tau)
    s=exp_c.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite:
       q=np.zeros_like(c); q[argmax]=1
       return q
    return exp_c/s
```
To avoid exp overflow when c/tau positive? We subtract max, so ≤0. Good. If tau very small and many equal max, okay. If tau < 1e-12*(range+1), just use top frac? Could set min tau=1e-4 * max_c maybe. For c max~1.5, 1e-4. q almost one-hot. The gradient G = ∑q p for one lag k is the vector a? For k, G_i=p_{k-i} if in range. This coordinate direction may be okay.

def run_mgd(a0, budget, tau0, tau_min, lr0, verbose):
    n=len; S=sqrt(2n); p=normalize_p(a0)
    best=a0... best_score=score
    lr=lr0; last_score=best_score; no_improve=0
    while time left:
       progress
       tau=tau0*(tau_min/tau0)**progress (ensure >1e-6)
       c_p = conv_square(p) # sum p=1
       max_c = 2n * c_p.max()
       q = soft_weights(2n*c_p, tau) # or c_p scaled, tau_scaled
       G = conv_q_p(q,p)
       h = G - G.mean()
       hscale = h.max() - h.min() (or np.ptp)
       if hscale < 1e-12: break or noise
       h = h / hscale
       # optional clip
       expo = -lr*h
       expo = np.clip(expo, -5,5)
       p_new = p * np.exp(expo)
       s=p_new.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue

       # optional floor
       if p_new.min() < 1e-15: p_new = p_new + 1e-15; p_new/=p_new.sum()
       p=p_new
       a = S*p
       score = max_c? We already have c_p from old p. Need new score. Could compute new c? We compute each loop at start, so after update we didn't compute. We can loop compute at top. `compute_conv_square_p(p)`:
- M = next_fast_len(2*n) maybe precomputed. To avoid global state, pass M.
```
def conv_square(x, M):
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, M)
    f = fx * fx
    y = np.fft.irfft(f, M)
    return y[:2*len(x)-1]
```
Because x real. For p sum1. For n=3000, M=8192. Good.

`compute_G(q,p,Mcorr)`:
```
def conv_q_p(q, p, M):
    # M >= len(p)+len(q)-1 =3n-2
    f_p = rfft(p, M)
    f_q = rfft(q[::-1], M)
    conv = irfft(f_p*f_q, M)
    # G_i = conv[2n-2 - i]
    if n==1: return np.array([q[0]*p[0]])
    idx_slice = slice(2*n-2, n-2, -1)
    return conv[idx_slice]
```
Need Mcorr maybe next pow2 ≥3n-2. For n=4096, Mcorr=32768. rfft length M returns M/2+1. Good. Advanced reversed convolution: q[::-1] creates copy length 8191; okay. Could precompute indices? Slice is good.
For n=1, slice weird; handle.

Potential numerical negative conv values small; set `conv = np.maximum(conv,0)`? G should nonnegative; clip to 0 to avoid small negative. `q` nonneg. `p` nonneg. Do `G = conv[...]; G = np.maximum(G, 0.0)`.

Now optimizer details:
```
def run_mgd(a0, budget_s, tau_hi=0.25, tau_lo=0.0005, lr0=0.05, lr_min=1e-4, lr_max=0.6, verbose=True):
    n=len(a0); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    p=as_prob(a0)
    a=S*p
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_from_p(p,n)
    Mconv=fft_len(2*n)
    Mgrad=fft_len(3*n - 1) #
    c = conv_square(p, Mconv)
    csc = (2.0*n) * c
    maxc = csc.max()
    tau=tau_hi
    lr=lr0
    prev=maxc
    no=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    step=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < budget_s:
       step+=1
       # tau schedule based on step or time? time. f=elapsed/budget
       f=elapsed/budget; tau = max(tau_lo, tau_hi * (tau_lo/tau_hi)**f)
       q=soft_weights(csc, tau)
       G=conv_q_p(q,p,Mgrad)
       h=G - np.mean(G)
       scale=np.max(np.abs(h))
       if scale<1e-12:
           # perturb?
           p = 0.999*p + 0.001/n? maybe
           break
       h = h / scale
       expo = -lr * h
       expo = np.clip(expo, -3.0, 3.0)
       pnew = p * np.exp(expo)
       s=pnew.sum()
       if s <=0 or not finite: pnew=p; lr*=0.5; continue
       pnew /= s
       # floor and smoothing (optional): keep positive
       # if min too small: pnew[pnew<1e-18]=1e-18; renorm
       p=pnew
       c=conv_square(p,Mconv)
       csc=2*n*c
       newmax=csc.max()
       if newmax < best_score - 1e-9:
            best_score=newmax; best=S*p.copy()
            # print occasional
       # adapt lr
       if newmax < prev - 1e-9:
           lr = min(lr_max, lr*1.05)
           no=0
       else:
           lr = max(lr_min, lr*0.9)
           no+=1
           if no > 200:
              # reduce tau early? multiply local_tau? Use tau_override *=0.5? Simpler: lr*0.5 and add perturbation
              no=0
              # add small random noise to p to escape
              rng=np.random.default_rng(int(step))
              noise=rng.normal(0,0.01,n)
              p = p + noise*np.median(p)
              p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
       prev = min(prev, newmax)? For line search? If newmax increases, prev should maybe newmax to adapt down. Use prev=newmax.
    return best, best_score
```

Implementation of `make_power_weights(c, alpha, eps=1e-12)`:
```
def make_power_weights(c, alpha):
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <= 0: return uniform
    x = c / cmax
    if alpha < 1e-3: return uniform
    # For numerical: use log if alpha large
    if alpha > 50:
        # use softmax-like on log(x/eps)
        lx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-15))
        w = np.exp((lx - lx.max()) * max(alpha/50? no)
```
If alpha large, x^alpha underflows except max. We can handle by clipping: `w = np.power(x + 1e-12, alpha)`; with alpha up to 100, min 1e-12^100 underflow, but okay (zeros). To preserve some weight, set floor `x = np.maximum(x, 1e-8)` before power; then alpha=100 floor 1e-80. Underflow to 0 maybe. Use `np.exp(alpha*np.log(x_floor))`; if exponent < -700 set 0. Good. That's fine. Normalize.
Could also use stable: `w = np.exp(alpha*(np.log(x_floor)-np.log(x_floor.max())))`; if exponent<-700 ->0.
Alpha up to maybe 200. Good.

Maybe weights normalized. If all underflow except max, q one-hot.

`run_pwr(a0, budget, gamma_start=0.5, gamma_end=100, lr0=0.05, lr_max=0.5, verbose=True)`:
- Initialize p from a0; compute c,csc, score.
- best = p*S, best_score.
- Set `old_p` for possible revert.
- t=0; no=0; lr=lr0.
- while time left:
   f=elapsed/budget
   gamma = gamma_start*(gamma_end/gamma_start)**f
   q=make_power_weights(csc, gamma)
   G=conv_q_p(q,p,Mgrad)
   h=G-G.mean(); scale=np.max(np.abs(h)); if scale small: break/noise
   no_update? h/=scale
   expo=clip(-lr*h, -4,4)
   pnew=p*exp(expo); normalize. Maybe mix with uniform floor `pnew = (1-1e-8)*pnew + 1e-8/n`? small.
   compute cnew, newmax.
   if newmax < prevmax - 1e-12:
       accept; lr=min(lrmax, lr*1.03); no=0; if newmax<best: update best
   else:
       # Try smaller half step? We can attempt if pnew bad, use previous p to try lr*0.5 from old c? But we already at old p. Could line search:
   Simpler: Accept if newmax < prevmax + 0.02; else revert.
       if newmax < prevmax + 0.05:
            accept p=pnew; c=cnew; csc=cscnew; lr=max(min, lr*0.8); no+=1
       else:
            lr=max(min, lr*0.6); # stay old
            no+= larger
       no...
   if no>100: # stuck
       # restart from best with random perturbations
       p = best_p copy or current? Use best.
       rng seed step
       noise scale maybe 0.05/np.sqrt(n)? Add small multiplicative noise: logpert = rng.normal(0, 0.1, n); p = p * exp(logpert - mean(logpert)); normalize.
       update c
       lr=lr0*0.5; no=0
   prevmax = newmax if accepted else prevmax?
- Return best.

Implement `score_seq(a)`:
```
def score_seq(a):
    n=len(a); s=a.sum()
    if n<=0 or s<0.01: return inf
    c=conv_square(a) # but actual a not normalized, c scale sum^2.
    return float(2*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```

Now testing root init O(n^2) maybe for n=8192 direct recurrence using Python `np.dot` with slicing each k. Let's code efficient:
```
def init_root_flat(n):
    a=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n==0: return a
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        if k==1:
            cross=0.0
        else:
            # sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}
            # use dot a[1:k] and a[k-1:0:-1]
            cross = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
        val=(1.0 - cross)/(2.0*a[0])
        if val < 0.0: val=0.0
        a[k]=val
    return a
```
For k=1, dot empty 0. For flat target 1. a0=1. Since a0 constant 1. If target scaled, unchanged. Could target not flat: pass target array. But use flat.

```
import numpy as np
import time
# try scipy.fft? maybe not needed
# use numpy.fft

def fft_len(length): ...
def as_probability(a): ...
def conv_square(x, M=None): if M None compute len.
def score_from_p(p): n=p.size; c=conv_square(p); return 2*n*c.max()
def make_power_weights(c, gamma): ...

def perturb_p(p, scale, rng):
   y=np.log(p + 1e-30)
   y += rng.normal(0, scale, size=n)
   y -= y.mean()
   pnew=np.exp(y - y.max()); pnew/=pnew.sum()

Let's refine `run_pwr`:
```
def run_pwr(a0, budget_s, gamma_start=1.0, gamma_end=80.0, lr0=0.15, lr_min=1e-4, lr_max=0.8, momentum=0.3, max_step=2.5, verbose=False):
    p=as_probability(a0); n=p.size
    S=np.sqrt(2n) not needed if return p; but score_from_p.
    Mconv=fft_len(2*n-1); Mgrad=fft_len(3*n-2)
    c=conv_square(p,Mconv); sc=score_from_c(c,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=sc
    vel=np.zeros(n)
    lr=lr0; no=0; last_best_time=time.monotonic(); t0=...
    while time budget:
       f=el/budget; gamma = gamma_start*(gamma_end/gamma_start)**f
       q=make_power_weights(c,gamma)
       G=conv_q_p(q,p,Mgrad)
       h=G-np.mean(G)
       scale=np.max(np.abs(h))
       if scale < 1e-12:
           # add tiny noise
           break
       h = h/scale
       step = -lr*h
       if momentum !=0:
           vel = momentum*vel + step
       else:
           vel = step
       if max_step: np.clip(vel, -max_step, max_step, out=vel)
       pnew = p*np.exp(vel)
       s=pnew.sum(); if not finite: vel=0; lr*=0.5; continue
       pnew/=s
       cnew=conv_square(pnew,Mconv); newsc=2n*cnew.max()
       # floor
       if newsc < prevsc + 0.02: # accept tolerance
           # if too large jump revert
           if newsc > best_score and newsc > prevsc + 0.1:
               lr *=0.7; vel*=0.5; continue
           p=pnew; c=cnew; prevsc=newsc
           if newsc < best_score - 1e-9:
               best_score=newsc; best_p=p.copy(); last_best_time=now
               if verbose print
           if newsc < prev_old: lr=min(lrmax,lr*1.03); no=0
           else: lr=max(lrmin,lr*0.9); no+=1
       else:
           pnew not accepted; vel*=0.5; lr=max(lrmin,lr*0.7); no+=5
       # stagnation time check
       if time - last_best_time > 60:
           # perturb from best
           p=perturb_log(best_p,0.15, np.random.default_rng(int(time*1000)%...))
           c=conv_square(p,Mconv); prevsc=2n*c.max()
           vel*=0.5; lr=max(lrmin, lr*0.7)
           last_best_time=time? maybe reset to allow more if still best.
       if no>300: more aggressive perturb
    return best_p, best_score
```

def run_freq(a0, budget, cap_frac=1.0, damp=0.5, gamma? ):
   p=as_prob; best...
   M=fft_len(2*n-1)
   c=conv_square(p,M); cmax=c.max()
   # target maybe c with cap at cmax? If cap=cmax no change. Need cap lower? We can set target = np.minimum(c, current_max_target) where current_max_target slightly lower than current max, e.g. 0.99*cmax or best_target? But lowering cap may not be feasible; frequency projection will try.
   while time:
      c=conv_square(p); Mcur=c.max()
      # target schedule: cap = max(Mcur - delta, lower_bound=avg*1.01)
      delta = 0.05*Mcur initially, decrease later.
      target = np.minimum(c, cap)
      T=rfft(target,M); Ccur=rfft(p,M); Ccur=Ccur*Ccur
      A_new = A * (1-damp) + damp * A*np.sqrt(T/Ccur)? Or A2 = A * sqrt(T/Ccur); A_new = (1-damp)*A + damp*A2
      p_new = irfft(A_new)[:n]; clip; normalize
      score; track best
   return best
```

Let's design `run_freq` robust:
```
def run_freq_capped(p0, budget_s, cap_factor_start=0.98, cap_factor_end=0.95, damp=0.5, verbose=False):
    p=asProb; n; M=fft_len(2n-1)
    c=conv_square(p,M); best_score=2n*c.max(); best_p=p.copy(); avg=1.0/(2n-1)
    t0; elapsed; 
    for step until budget:
       f=elapsed/budget
       cap_scale = cap_factor_start + (cap_factor_end - cap_factor_start)*f
       Mcur=c.max()
       cap = max(avg*1.01, Mcur*cap_scale)
       # Need target length 2n-1, sum maybe? If we lower high c, sum target < sum c=1, frequency projection will scale? p sum1; target sum not 1, but okay? It may change scale? A_new = A*sqrt(T/Ccur). Product A_new^2 = T? If T sum less than1, resulting p before clip sum maybe not 1; normalize sum after clip. This is okay.
       target=np.minimum(c, cap)
       A=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
       C=A*A
       # safe division
       Cabs=np.abs(C)
       ratio = np.empty_like(C, dtype=complex)
       mask=Cabs>1e-14
       ratio[mask]=np.fft.rfft(target,M)[mask]/C[mask]
       ratio[~mask]=1+0j
       A2=A*np.sqrt(ratio)
       Anew=(1-damp)*A + damp*A2
       pnew=np.fft.irfft(Anew,M)[:n].real
       pnew=np.maximum(pnew,0)
       s=pnew.sum(); if s>0: pnew/=s else pnew=p
       c=conv_square(pnew,M); newsc=2n*c.max()
       if newsc < best_score: update
       # adapt damp maybe damp = 0.5 +0.3f
    return best_p,best_score
```
Potential issue: `np.sqrt(ratio)` on complex with negative real can produce arbitrary phase; damping. It may introduce oscillations; clip. This is exploratory.
Could run with small budget. If it doesn't improve, no harm.

Potential bugs:
- `conv_square` with M default. For `run_freq`, M power2 enough for 2n-1. For gradient Mgrad power2 >=3n-2. `fft_len` power of 2. If n=1? Not likely but handle.
- `as_probability`: if a has sum <=0, return uniform. Ensure finite.
```
def as_probability(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(a.size,1))
    p=a/s
    # ensure no zeros? leave
    return p
```

```
def make_power_weights(c, gamma):
    n=c.size
    cmax=np.max(c)
    if cmax <= 0 or not finite: return np.full(n,1/n)
    x=c/cmax
    floor=1e-12
    x=np.maximum(x, floor)
    # if gamma < 0.01 uniform
    if gamma < 1e-6: return uniform
    logx=np.log(x)

    exp_arg = gamma*(logx - np.max(logx))
    # if exp_arg < -700 -> 0

    # For non-mask, w=0. But at max w=1.
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=np.full(n,1/n)
    else: w/=s
    return w

def conv_square(x, M=None):
   n=x.size
   if M is None: M=fft_len(2*n-1)
   fx=np.fft.rfft(x, M)
   y=np.fft.irfft(fx*fx, M)

`resample_seq`:
```
def resample_seq(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a).ravel(); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if a.size==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size,endpoint=True)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=True)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a),0.0)
```

`init_chirp` to generate smooth oscillatory:
```
def init_fourier(n, seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i=np.arange(n)
    a=np.ones(n)
    for k in range(1,4):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)
       freq=rng.choice([1,2,3,5])
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       a += amp*np.cos(freq*(2*np.pi*i/(n-1)) + phase)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.1)
    return a
```

`perturb_log`:
```
def perturb_log(p, scale, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    y=np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    noise=rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=p.size)
    y = y + noise
    y = y - np.mean(y)
    pnew=np.exp(y - np.max(y))
    s=pnew.sum()
    if s>0: pnew/=s
    else: pnew=p.copy()
    return pnew
```

```
def run_pwr(a0, budget_s, gamma_start=1.0, gamma_end=80.0, lr0=0.12, lr_min=1e-4, lr_max=0.6, momentum=0.35, max_step=2.0, verbose=False):
    p=as_probability(a0)
    n=p.size
    if n<=0: return p, np.inf
    Mconv=fft_len(2*n-1)
    Mgrad=fft_len(3*n-2)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    remaining_budget=max(1e-3,budget_s)
    c=conv_square(p,Mconv)
    sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=sc; best_time=t0
    vel=np.zeros(n)
    lr=lr0
    prev_sc=sc
    no_global=0
    last_improve=t0
    step=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < remaining_budget:
       now=time.monotonic(); el=now-t0
       if el >= remaining_budget: break
       f = el / remaining_budget
       if f<0: f=0; if f>1: f=1
       gamma = gamma_start * (gamma_end/gamma_start)**f
       q=make_power_weights(c, gamma)
       G=conv_q_p(q,p,Mgrad)
       h=G-np.mean(G)
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if scale <= 1e-12:
           # add small noise
           p=perturb_log(p,0.05, 555+step)
           c=conv_square(p,Mconv); prev_sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
           vel.fill(0.0)
           continue
       h = h/scale
       step_est = -lr*h
       if momentum > 0:
           vel = momentum*vel + step_est
       else:
           vel = step_est
       if max_step > 0:
           np.clip(vel, -max_step, max_step, out=vel)
       pnew = p * np.exp(vel)
       s = pnew.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
           vel*=0.5; lr=max(lr_min, lr*0.5); continue
       pnew /= s
       # tiny floor? if min pnew ==0, not needed. Could do pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 1e-300); renorm
       cnew = conv_square(pnew, Mconv)
       new_sc = 2.0*n*float(np.max(cnew))
       improved_global = new_sc < best_score - 1e-10
       accept = new_sc <= prev_sc + 0.004 + 1e-8
       if accept:
           # if too high vs best, maybe revert if far
           if new_sc > best_score + 0.05:
               accept=False
       if accept:
           p=pnew; c=cnew; prev_sc=new_sc
           if improved_global:
               best_score=new_sc; best_p=pnew.copy(); best_time=now; last_improve=now; no_global=0
               if verbose: print(...)
           if new_sc < prev_old - 1e-10: # need prev_old before overwrite
```

def run_freq_capped(p0, budget_s, start_scale=0.98, end_scale=0.95, damp=0.6, verbose=False):
    p=as_probability(p0); n; M=fft_len(2*n-1); avg=1.0/(2*n-1)
    t0=...
    c=conv_square(p,M); best=...; prev=best;
    while time:
       f=...
       scale = start_scale + (end_scale-start_scale)*f
       Mcur=2n*c.max? Actually c max p. cap = max(avg*1.01, c.max()*scale)
       target = np.minimum(c, cap)
       A=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
       C=A*A
       T=np.fft.rfft(target,M)
       Cabs=np.abs(C)
       ratio=np.ones_like(C, dtype=np.complex128)
       mask=Cabs > 1e-14
       ratio[mask] = T[mask]/C[mask]
       # To keep branch, maybe use np.sqrt(ratio[mask], out=...)
       # Use damping in A space:
       A2 = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
       Anew = (1.0-damp)*A + damp*A2
       pnew = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n].real
       pnew=np.maximum(pnew,0.0)
       s=pnew.sum(); if s>0: pnew/=s; else pnew=p.copy()

       if newsc < prev_sc - 1e-9 or newsc < prev_sc + 0.002:
           p=pnew; c=cnew; prev_sc=newsc
           if newsc<best: update
       else:
           # reduce damp? 
           damp *=0.7 (if <0.1 reset?)
       # maybe if stagnate, update target flat? 
    return best_p,best_score

Let's code `make_power_weights` carefully:
```
def make_power_weights(c, gamma):
    m = c.size
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if m <=0 or cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.full(m, 1.0/m if m else 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = c / cmax
    # Ensure nonnegative finite
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x > 0.0), x, 1e-12)
    floor = 1e-12
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    if gamma <= 1e-6:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0/m, dtype=np.float64)
        return w
    logx = np.log(x)
    maxlog = float(np.max(logx))
    ex = gamma * (logx - maxlog)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = ex > -700.0
    w[mask] = np.exp(ex[mask])
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        # fallback top values
        idx = np.argmax(c)
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64); w[idx]=1.0
        return w
    w /= s
    return w
```
If many max values, exponent 0 for them; weights equal. Good.

`conv_q_p` potential negative due FFT roundoff; `G = np.maximum(G,0)`.
Use `q[::-1]` creates copy; okay. If q length 2n-1. For n large, q[::-1] negative step copy. Fine.

`run_freq_capped`: Need Damp adaptation. Let's code robust:
```
def run_freq_capped(p0, budget_s, start_scale=0.99, end_scale=0.95, damp0=0.6, verbose=False):
    p=as_probability(p0); n=p.size; M=fft_len(2*n-1); avg=1/(2n-1)
    c=conv_square(p,M); sc=2n max
    best...
    prev=sc
    damp=damp0
    t0=...
    stagnate= time.monotonic()
    last_good=stagnate
    step=0
    while time<t0+budget:
       f=...
       scale = start_scale + (end_scale-start_scale)*f
       cap = max(avg*1.005, float(np.max(c))*scale)
       target = np.minimum(c, cap)
       A = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
       C = A*A
       T = np.fft.rfft(target, M)
       ratio = np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
       Cabs = np.abs(C)
       mask = Cabs > 1e-14
       ratio[mask] = T[mask] / C[mask]
       # guard finite
       ratio[~np.isfinite(ratio)] = 1+0j
       A2 = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
       Anew = (1.0 - damp)*A + damp*A2
       # Ensure conjugate symmetry? irfft expects; Anew from rfft computations should be okay but maybe small imag? Anew is complex array length M//2+1. irfft ignores imaginary? It uses real as positive frequencies; does not enforce symmetry explicitly. Good.
       pnew = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n].real
       pnew = np.maximum(pnew,0.0)
       s=pnew.sum()
       if s>0 and finite: pnew/=s else pnew=p
       cnew=conv_square(pnew,M); newsc=...
       if newsc < prev_sc - 1e-10 or newsc < prev_sc + 0.002:
           p=pnew; c=cnew; prev_sc=newsc
           if newsc < best_score - 1e-10:
               update; last_good=now
           damp = min(0.9, damp*1.02)
       else:
           damp = max(0.2, damp*0.75)
       if now - last_good > min(30.0, 0.2*budget):
           # try smaller cap scale or restart
           p=perturb_log(best_p,0.1, 777+step)
           c=conv_square(p,M); prev_sc=...
           damp=0.5
           last_good=now
       step+=1
    return best
```
If ratio T/C can be zero where target 0 but current C nonzero; sqrt 0, A2 zero at frequencies, damping. Target has zeros if c has zeros? p full positive c likely no zeros but maybe small. OK.
Potential: If A has near-zero positive frequencies, C near zero, mask avoids division; ratio=1, no change there.

`fft_len(length)`:
```
def fft_len(length):
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    m = 1
    while m < int(length):
        m <<= 1
    return m
```

`as_probability`:
```
def as_probability(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size ==0:
       return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    n=arr.size
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n, dtype=np.float64)
    p=arr/s
    floor=max(1e-12, 1e-9/n) # 1e-9/n maybe 1e-13 for 4096. max with 1e-12 ->1e-12.
    p=p+floor
    p=p/float(p.sum())
    return p
```
Adding floor 1e-12 changes sum negligible.

`init_root_flat(n)`:
```
def init_root_flat(n):
    a=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <=0: return a
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        if k == 1:
            cross=0.0
        else:
            cross=float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
        val=(1.0-cross)/2.0
        if val <0.0 or not np.isfinite(val): val=0.0
        a[k]=val
    return a
```
Since a0=1. For flat target 1. If target scaling? no.
Potential: For k large, cross might be slightly >1 due truncated/roundoff; set 0. But root values remain positive until maybe? okay.

`init_fourier`:
```
def init_fourier(n, seed=0):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(max(n,1))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom=float(n-1)
    a=np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(4):
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.04,0.25))
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,6)) # scipy? rng.integers returns. Use int.
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       a += amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*i/denom + phase)
    a=np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    return a
```
`np.pi` okay.

`init_inverse_sqrt`:
```
def init_inverse_sqrt(n):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return 1.0/np.sqrt(i+1.0)
```

def build_candidates(init_budget_s):

             if np.isfinite(sc): cands.append((sc,p,'provided_resamp_%d'%L))
          except Exception: pass
    if not cands:
       p=np.full(2048,1.0/2048)
       cands.append((score_from_p(p),p,'uniform'))
    return cands

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()

    cands.sort(key=cand_sort_key)

    print top candidates
    # Quick exploration top 3
    quick_n = min(3, len(cands))
    for idx in range(quick_n):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 90: break
       t_each = min(160.0, max(60.0, rem*0.07))

       p_new, sc = run_pwr(p, t_each, gamma_start=1.0, gamma_end=60.0, lr0=0.12, lr_max=0.6, verbose=True)
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=p_new
    # polish
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150:
       t1 = min(rem*0.5, 1000.0)
       p_new, sc = run_pwr(best_p, t1, gamma_start=0.8, gamma_end=40.0, lr0=0.10, lr_max=0.5, verbose=True)

       update
    rem=...
    if rem > 80:
       t3 = min(rem*0.75, 600.0)
       p_new,sc=run_freq_capped(best_p,t3,0.985,0.955,0.5,verbose=True)

       update
    # final evaluate with evaluate_sequence
    lst = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    try:
       es = evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if np.isfinite(es): print final; return lst
    except Exception:
       pass
    # if evaluate fails, ensure sum >=0.01
    s=sum(lst)
    if s < 0.01:
       lst = [0.1/len(lst)]*len(lst)
    return lst

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program uses several initializations (provided sequence, one-sided
square-root-of-flat-profile, tilted roots, smooth random/Fourier shapes) and
then optimizes them with:
  * multiplicative / log-domain projected descent using power-controlled
    active-weights (a generalization of smooth-max), and
  * a frequency-domain capped-convolution projection.

All helper functions are top-level.  No lambdas are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def fft_len(length):
    """Smallest power-of-two FFT length at least length."""
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    m = 1
    while m < int(length):
        m <<= 1
    return m


def as_probability(a):
    """Convert an array to a strict positive probability vector (sum = 1)."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())

    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    p = arr / s

    # Keep every coordinate strictly positive.
    floor = max(1e-12, 1e-10 / float(n))
    p = p + floor
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    return p


def conv_square(x, M=None):
    """Linear convolution x * x using zero-padded FFT."""
    n = int(np.size(x))
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if M is None:
        M = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, M)
    y = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, M)
    return y[:2 * n - 1]


def score_from_p(p):
    """Score for p with sum(p) = 1: 2*n*max(conv(p,p))."""
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    M = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    c = conv_square(p, M)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * m)


def make_power_weights(c, gamma):
    """
    Positive weights q_k proportional to max(c_k, eps)^gamma.
    Large gamma focuses on the current peaks; small gamma flattens.
    """
    m = int(c.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)

    x = c / cmax
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x > 0.0), x, 1e-12)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-12)

    if gamma <= 1e-8:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
        return w

    logx = np.log(x)
    maxlog = float(np.max(logx))
    ex = gamma * (logx - maxlog)

    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = ex > -700.0
    w[mask] = np.exp(ex[mask])

    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        # Fallback: put weight on current maxima.
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w

    w /= s
    return w


def conv_q_p(q, p, M):
    """
    G_i = sum_j q_{i+j} p_j, for i = 0,...,n-1.
    This is the matrix-vector product appearing in the gradient of
    sum_k q_k conv(p,p)_k.
    """
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(q[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    fq = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], M)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fq * fp, M)

    G = conv[2 * n - 2: n - 2: -1]
    G = np.maximum(G, 0.0)
    return G


def perturb_log(p, scale, seed):
    """Multiplicative log-Gaussian perturbation, preserving positivity/sum."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    y = y + rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=p.size)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    pnew = np.exp(y - float(np.max(y)))
    s = float(pnew.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return p.copy()
    pnew /= s
    return pnew


def run_pwr(a0, budget_s, gamma_start=1.0, gamma_end=80.0, lr0=0.25,
            lr_min=1e-4, lr_max=1.0, momentum=0.30, max_step=2.0,
            verbose=False, seed_base=0):
    """
    Log-domain multiplicative descent using power weights on conv(p,p).
    p is kept normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if budget_s <= 1e-3:
        p = as_probability(a0)
        return p, score_from_p(p)

    p = as_probability(a0)
    n = int(p.size)
    Mconv = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    Mgrad = fft_len(3 * n - 2)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c = conv_square(p, Mconv)
    sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = sc
    last_best = t0
    last_perturb = t0
    prev_sc = sc

    lr = float(lr0)
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    no_global = 0
    step = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - t0
        if elapsed >= budget_s:
            break

        f = elapsed / budget_s
        if f < 0.0:
            f = 0.0
        if f > 1.0:
            f = 1.0

        gamma = gamma_start * (gamma_end / gamma_start) ** f
        q = make_power_weights(c, gamma)
        G = conv_q_p(q, p, Mgrad)

        h = G - float(np.mean(G))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))

        if scale <= 1e-12:
            p = perturb_log(p, 0.08, int(seed_base + 100000 + step * 13))
            c = conv_square(p, Mconv)
            prev_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            step += 1
            continue

        hn = h / scale
        step_vec = -lr * hn

        if momentum > 0.0:
            vel = momentum * vel + step_vec
        else:
            vel = step_vec

        if max_step > 0.0:
            np.clip(vel, -max_step, max_step, out=vel)

        pnew = p * np.exp(vel)
        s = float(pnew.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            vel *= 0.5
            lr = max(lr_min, 0.5 * lr)
            step += 1
            continue

        pnew /= s
        cnew = conv_square(pnew, Mconv)
        new_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(cnew)))

        old_sc = prev_sc
        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < old_sc - 1e-10:
                accept = True
            else:
                accept = (new_sc <= old_sc + 0.003) and (new_sc <= best_score + 0.02)

        if accept:
            p = pnew
            c = cnew
            prev_sc = new_sc

            if new_sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_score = new_sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()
                no_global = 0
                if verbose:
                    print(f"      pwr improve: n={n} step={step} score={new_sc:.6f} gamma={gamma:.3f}", flush=True)

            if new_sc < old_sc - 1e-10:
                lr = min(lr_max, lr * 1.04)
            else:
                lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.92)
                no_global += 1
        else:
            vel *= 0.5
            lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.72)
            no_global += 3

        # Time-based restart from best if stuck.
        if (time.monotonic() - last_best > min(60.0, 0.30 * budget_s) and
                time.monotonic() - last_perturb > min(30.0, 0.15 * budget_s)):
            scale_perturb = 0.12 + 0.08 * min(1.0, no_global / 250.0)
            p = perturb_log(best_p, scale_perturb, int(seed_base + 777777 + step * 17))
            c = conv_square(p, Mconv)
            prev_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))
            vel *= 0.25
            lr = max(lr_min, min(lr0 * 0.8, lr))
            no_global = 0
            last_perturb = time.monotonic()

        # Hard reset if stuck long even without time threshold.
        if no_global > 500:
            p = perturb_log(best_p, 0.25, int(seed_base + 888888 + step * 23))
            c = conv_square(p, Mconv)
            prev_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            lr = max(lr_min, 0.5 * lr0)
            no_global = 0
            last_perturb = time.monotonic()

        if verbose and step % 75 == 0:
            print(f"      pwr step={step} n={n} score={prev_sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} gamma={gamma:.3f} lr={lr:.4f}",
                  flush=True)

        step += 1

    if verbose:
        print(f"      pwr done: n={p.size} best={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    return best_p, best_score


def run_freq_capped(a0, budget_s, start_scale=0.985, end_scale=0.955,
                    damp0=0.55, verbose=False, seed_base=0):
    """
    Frequency-domain projection toward a capped version of the current
    auto-convolution.  Useful as a final flattening step.
    """
    if budget_s <= 1e-3:
        p = as_probability(a0)
        return p, score_from_p(p)

    p = as_probability(a0)
    n = int(p.size)
    M = fft_len(2 * n - 1)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c = conv_square(p, M)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))
    prev_sc = best_score
    last_best = t0
    last_perturb = t0
    damp = float(damp0)
    step = 0

    avg = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - t0
        if elapsed >= budget_s:
            break

        f = elapsed / budget_s
        if f < 0.0:
            f = 0.0
        if f > 1.0:
            f = 1.0

        scale = start_scale + (end_scale - start_scale) * f
        cur_max = float(np.max(c))
        cap = max(avg * 1.005, cur_max * scale)
        target = np.minimum(c, cap)

        A = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
        C = A * A
        T = np.fft.rfft(target, M)

        Cabs = np.abs(C)
        ratio = np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
        mask = Cabs > 1e-14
        ratio[mask] = T[mask] / C[mask]

        bad = ~np.isfinite(ratio)
        if np.any(bad):
            ratio[bad] = 1.0 + 0.0j

        A2 = A * np.sqrt(ratio)
        Anew = (1.0 - damp) * A + damp * A2

        pnew = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n].real
        pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 0.0)
        s = float(pnew.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            pnew = p.copy()
            damp = max(0.2, damp * 0.8)
        else:
            pnew /= s

        cnew = conv_square(pnew, M)
        new_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(cnew)))

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(new_sc):
            if new_sc < prev_sc - 1e-10:
                accept = True
            else:
                accept = (new_sc <= prev_sc + 0.0025) and (new_sc <= best_score + 0.02)

        if accept:
            p = pnew
            c = cnew
            prev_sc = new_sc
            if new_sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_score = new_sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()
                if verbose:
                    print(f"      freq improve: n={n} step={step} score={new_sc:.6f}", flush=True)
            if new_sc < prev_sc + 1e-9:
                damp = min(0.85, damp * 1.03)
            else:
                damp = max(0.20, damp * 0.93)
        else:
            damp = max(0.15, damp * 0.85)

        if (time.monotonic() - last_best > min(40.0, 0.25 * budget_s) and
                time.monotonic() - last_perturb > min(20.0, 0.12 * budget_s)):
            p = perturb_log(best_p, 0.10, int(seed_base + 999999 + step * 31))
            c = conv_square(p, M)
            prev_sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))
            damp = 0.55
            last_perturb = time.monotonic()

        if verbose and step % 25 == 0:
            print(f"      freq step={step} n={n} score={prev_sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} damp={damp:.3f}",
                  flush=True)

        step += 1

    return best_p, best_score


def init_root_flat(n):
    """
    One-sided square root of the flat sequence 1,2,...:
    coefficients a_k chosen so that first n convolution coefficients are 1.
    """
    a = np.zeros(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if int(n) <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, int(n)):
        if k == 1:
            cross = 0.0
        else:
            cross = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k - 1:0:-1]))
        val = (1.0 - cross) / 2.0
        if val < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(val):
            val = 0.0
        a[k] = val
    return a


def tilt_sequence(a, alpha):
    """Multiply by 1 + alpha*(i/(n-1)), keeping nonnegativity."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    w = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.maximum(a * w, 0.0)


def init_inverse_sqrt(n):
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return 1.0 / np.sqrt(i + 1.0)


def init_fourier(n, seed=0):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = float(n - 1)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(4):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.28))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * i / denom + phase)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    return a


def resample_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = max(1, int(n))

    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=True)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def cand_sort_key(item):
    return (float(item[0]), -int(item[1].size), str(item[2]))


def build_candidates(init_budget_s):
    """Create a diverse set of initialized probability vectors."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(init_budget_s))
    cands = []

    prov = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        p = as_probability(raw)
        if p.size >= 8:
            sc = score_from_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append((sc, p, "provided"))
            prov = p
    except Exception:
        prov = None

    lengths = []
    if prov is not None:
        lengths.append(int(prov.size))

    for L in (2048, 3000, 4096, 6144):
        if L not in lengths:
            lengths.append(L)

    filtered = []
    for L in lengths:
        if 128 <= L <= 6144 and L not in filtered:
            filtered.append(L)
    lengths = sorted(filtered)

    for L in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break

        # Root construction and simple tilts.
        try:
            root_a = init_root_flat(L)

            p = as_probability(root_a)
            sc = score_from_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append((sc, p, "root_%d" % L))

            for alpha in (0.22, -0.22, 0.45):
                a = tilt_sequence(root_a, alpha)
                p = as_probability(a)
                sc = score_from_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cands.append((sc, p, "roottilt_%d_a%.2f" % (L, alpha)))
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
            break

        try:
            a = init_inverse_sqrt(L)
            p = as_probability(a)
            sc = score_from_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append((sc, p, "isqrt_%d" % L))
        except Exception:
            pass

        for sd in (0, 1):
            try:
                a = init_fourier(L, sd)
                p = as_probability(a)
                sc = score_from_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cands.append((sc, p, "fourier_%d_%d" % (L, sd)))
            except Exception:
                pass

        if prov is not None and int(prov.size) != L:
            try:
                a = resample_seq(prov, L)
                p = as_probability(a)
                sc = score_from_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cands.append((sc, p, "provresamp_%d" % L))
            except Exception:
                pass

    if not cands:
        p = np.full(2048, 1.0 / 2048.0, dtype=np.float64)
        cands.append((score_from_p(p), p, "uniform"))

    cands.sort(key=cand_sort_key)
    return cands


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    if not np.isfinite(seed):
        seed = 42
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or float(budget_s) <= 0.0:
        budget_s = 2350.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_b = min(2380.0, max(30.0, float(budget_s)))
    margin = min(25.0, 0.10 * total_b)
    deadline = t_start + (total_b - margin)
    if deadline <= t_start:
        deadline = t_start + max(1.0, total_b - 1.0)

    print("propose_candidate: total budget %.1fs, deadline margin %.1fs" % (total_b, margin), flush=True)

    # Candidate generation.
    init_budget = min(180.0, max(20.0, 0.12 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
    cands = build_candidates(init_budget)

    if not cands:
        p = np.full(2048, 1.0 / 2048.0, dtype=np.float64)
        cands = [(score_from_p(p), p, "fallback_uniform")]

    # Choose a few distinct initializations, favoring different lengths.
    selected = []
    seen_len = set()
    for item in cands:
        L = int(item[1].size)
        if L in seen_len:
            continue
        seen_len.add(L)
        selected.append(item)
        if len(selected) >= 3:
            break

    for item in cands:
        if item not in selected:
            selected.append(item)
        if len(selected) >= 4:
            break

    best_item = selected[0]
    best_p = best_item[1].copy()
    best_score = float(best_item[0])

    print("best initial: score=%.6f n=%d label=%s" % (best_score, int(best_p.size), str(best_item[2])), flush=True)
    for k, item in enumerate(selected[:6]):
        print("  cand[%d] score=%.6f n=%d label=%s" % (k, float(item[0]), int(item[1].size), str(item[2])), flush=True)

    # Quick exploration of the most promising diverse starts.
    n_quick = min(3, len(selected))
    for i in range(n_quick):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        t_each = min(170.0, max(55.0, 0.075 * rem))
        p0, sc0, lab = selected[i]
        print("quick run %d/%d: n=%d init_score=%.6f time=%.1fs" % (i + 1, n_quick, int(p0.size), sc0, t_each), flush=True)

        try:
            p_new, sc_new = run_pwr(p0, t_each,
                                    gamma_start=1.0, gamma_end=70.0,
                                    lr0=0.25, lr_max=1.0,
                                    momentum=0.30, max_step=2.0,
                                    verbose=True, seed_base=int(seed) + 1000 * i)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("  quick updated global best: %.6f n=%d" % (best_score, int(best_p.size)), flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("  quick run failed:", exc, flush=True)

    # Moderate-gamma polish of current best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 140.0:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.48, 1100.0)
        print("polish moderate gamma: n=%d time=%.1fs" % (int(best_p.size), t1), flush=True)
        try:
            p_new, sc_new = run_pwr(best_p, t1,
                                    gamma_start=0.8, gamma_end=50.0,
                                    lr0=0.18, lr_max=0.8,
                                    momentum=0.30, max_step=1.8,
                                    verbose=True, seed_base=int(seed) + 20000)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("  polish moderate updated best: %.6f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("polish moderate failed:", exc, flush=True)

    # High-gamma peak polishing.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 140.0:
        t2 = min(rem * 0.55, 1000.0)
        print("polish high gamma: n=%d time=%.1fs" % (int(best_p.size), t2), flush=True)
        try:
            p_new, sc_new = run_pwr(best_p, t2,
                                    gamma_start=8.0, gamma_end=140.0,
                                    lr0=0.09, lr_max=0.45,
                                    momentum=0.25, max_step=1.2,
                                    verbose=True, seed_base=int(seed) + 30000)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("  polish high updated best: %.6f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("polish high failed:", exc, flush=True)

    # Frequency capped-convolution projection.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        t3 = min(rem * 0.72, 600.0)
        print("frequency projection: n=%d time=%.1fs" % (int(best_p.size), t3), flush=True)
        try:
            p_new, sc_new = run_freq_capped(best_p, t3,
                                            start_scale=0.985, end_scale=0.955,
                                            damp0=0.55, verbose=True,
                                            seed_base=int(seed) + 40000)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("  frequency updated best: %.6f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("frequency projection failed:", exc, flush=True)

    # Final tiny peak-focused pass.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0:
        t4 = min(rem * 0.80, 300.0)
        print("final peak pass: n=%d time=%.1fs" % (int(best_p.size), t4), flush=True)
        try:
            p_new, sc_new = run_pwr(best_p, t4,
                                    gamma_start=20.0, gamma_end=220.0,
                                    lr0=0.05, lr_max=0.30,
                                    momentum=0.20, max_step=0.9,
                                    verbose=True, seed_base=int(seed) + 50000)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("  final pass updated best: %.6f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final pass failed:", exc, flush=True)

    # Sanity check: force strict positive, sum about 1.
    try:
        best_p = as_probability(best_p)
    except Exception:
        best_p = np.full(2048, 1.0 / 2048.0, dtype=np.float64)

    # Validate with official evaluator if available.
    out_list = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
        if np.isfinite(official):
            print("FINAL internal score=%.6f official=%s" % (best_score, official), flush=True)
        else:
            print("FINAL internal score=%.6f official non-finite; keeping internal best" % best_score, flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("FINAL internal score=%.6f (official evaluator not checked)" % best_score, flush=True)

    return out_list
```
